PoS-on-GA v13 complete public plain-text handoff. Exact public member bytes are separately available in raw_v10, raw_compat_v2 and acceptance_v13. Each section records the source byte hash; no provider authentication, endpoint or hidden thinking is included.

===== BEGIN AUDIT_V13_ROLE_CONTRACT_STOP.txt bytes=8670 sha256=40c96935677a91b423c6c3cc296cdc05ff0301784c9d688109a176bb210f418d =====
PoS-on-GA JSON-role contract adaptation and stop audit (2026-10-18)

Outcome
The bounded adaptation, committed-code offline checks, and one fresh zero-real-
model native-SHARED Docker synthetic certification passed. The one authorized
new real PoS compatibility check failed under the released PoS parser/schema.
Therefore nginx-request-logging, adaptive-rejection-sampler, sqlite-with-gcov,
and write-compressor were prepared but not started. No reward exists for them;
unknown is not zero. This audit is not a PoS efficacy or PMA comparison.

Identity and separation of runs
- Baseline: 0f96b40b90c62a8de05357635ca387906b40cc43.
- Frozen runtime/role-probe/serial code commit: 6f82ab94. Exact source hashes
  appear in raw_compat_v2/preparation/authorized_serial_scope.json and
  raw_compat_v2/probe/identity.json. Actual staged worker bytes and Git blob
  identities are separately certified in raw_v10/normal/execution_identity.json.
- Upstream PoS vendor remains luoyu100/PoS@6818cfa6434dbc91fcf67f0b3eb12a91db47433f;
  no vendor, prompt core, Sentinel, health, recovery, Task client, PMA, official
  task or Harbor method file was changed by this adaptation.
- Fixed official task source 2fd12b88aafdd04a52c298e3940bcb189f9766d6;
  Harbor 459ff6ec99417589b7f679d14ddf3b3f0ae4f1dc. The four prepared
  manifests contain per-task image, task, evaluator and source hashes. The
  current scope freezes their order and 300-turn/14400-second Task budget.
- The historical GA two-step protocol check and failed PoS probe remain in
  raw_compat_v1. The GA calls were NOT repeated in this batch. The two old
  public belief answers were replayed read-only in test_role_contract.py;
  their original failed response records were not overwritten.
- The new Docker synthetic is raw_v10/ (43 readable files; run
  pos-29720eac309b4a65). It ran one synthetic Task tool and one synthetic
  sentinel, with zero real model and zero official evaluator calls. It was
  executed once. The earlier unchanged sentinel negative cases were not rerun.

Exact adaptation contract
- Only PoS belief/consistency/progress role callers use
  pos-role-json-object-fence-v1. A plain response is passed byte-for-byte
  unchanged to the original parser, even if invalid. For an entire response
  matching exactly one triple-backtick JSON (or unlabeled) fence, with LF or
  CRLF line endings and only spaces/tabs/CR/LF outside, the adapter checks
  that the interior is exactly one strict JSON object. It then removes only
  the opening/closing fence and allowed outer whitespace. The interior string
  is not reserialized or repaired. Duplicate keys, nonstandard constants,
  nonfinite numbers, non-object roots, trailing data, partial data, multiple
  fences and explanatory prose are not accepted as the new path. They pass
  unchanged to the original parser and its original bounded correction.
- The provider's public response is retained in pos_role_response.response.
  A separate pos_role_json_adapter event has the contract version, role/call
  identity, raw/parser strings and SHA-256, transform and removed boundary.
  Original upstream raw_response fields mean the string actually handed to
  their parser, which may be adapted; they must not be called provider-raw.
  Hidden thinking is excluded from this output and public archive.
- PoS role configuration COPIES now explicitly use thinking_type=disabled,
  temperature=0, belief max_tokens=4096, consistency/progress=512, and zero
  transport retry. Model remains claude-opus-4-8 with the previously frozen
  private route/profile bytes. These settings were NOT present in the prior
  failed live probe. Task settings and native tool behavior are unchanged.
  No unverified response_format parameter, tool-forced JSON, extra LLM or
  vendor modification was added. See the new manifest config/config_sha256.

Offline evidence
- acceptance_v13/role_committed.txt: 6 tests pass. The two old raw answers
  still fail direct json.loads, while their exact interiors pass the original
  initialization validator after only outer-fence removal. The test also
  exercises init, update, textualization, consistency and progress through
  the real PoS caller seam; a rejected duplicate-key fence reaches the
  original parser and its one correction. Ambiguous/invalid cases remain raw.
- acceptance_v13/role_http_payloads.json is an actual raw_ask -> patched
  requests.post capture: PoS payloads show disabled thinking, temperature 0,
  budgets 4096/512/512. The same unmodified Task profile produces its old
  adaptive-thinking/default-temperature payload. No provider was called.
- Committed-code regressions: offline 6/6, GA seam 18/18, scoped staging 2/2,
  terminal handoff 1/1, live wiring 7/7. Original stdout/stderr are in
  acceptance_v13/. Two earlier module-style invocations failed import
  setup; the failure logs were retained as offline_tests.txt and
  ga_seam_tests.txt. Script-style reruns passed; failures were not counted
  as passes. The complete bounded code/test diff is DIFF_V13.patch.
- The new synthetic raw_v10 committed certification returned pass: 43 files,
  actual Task CURRENT_TASK_DONE, bound worker receipt/graceful exit, same-main
  stop-writer release, one native synthetic sentinel execution, cleanup true.

One new real compatibility check and its failure
- Prepared scope: raw_compat_v2/preparation/authorized_serial_scope.json,
  bound to code commit 1449182ee39b8d4c39d7a8e066cc406f4091aee5 and the
  new committed certification. Four separate manifests/run IDs exist, but
  formal_runs_started=0. No per-task one-use authorization was created.
- Probe: raw_compat_v2/probe/. It issued no GA Task request and no Task tool;
  only one PoS reset followed by one synthetic nonterminal update attempt.
  It made 6 role logical calls and 6 observable HTTP attempts: belief x4,
  consistency x2, progress x0. All six actual requests asked for
  claude-opus-4-8, thinking disabled and temperature 0; 4096/512 budgets
  match their roles. Every HTTP response was 200, returned model identity was
  claude-opus-4-8, and stop reason was end_turn. Role provider usage per call
  is retained in role_events.jsonl and HTTP usage events in
  http_attempts.json; no price total is inferred.
- Six adapter events occurred: 4 outer_fence_removed, 2 unchanged. Reset's
  initial belief and belief_text parsed; an initialized partial belief state
  was retained. Both consistency replies were a fenced JSON object PLUS
  explanatory prose. The strict adapter correctly left them unchanged;
  original Sentinel recorded two parse errors/audit failure. During update,
  two fully fenced belief objects had their outer fence removed, but both
  failed the released schema validator (two belief_model_schema_error events).
  The original bounded correction was consumed; final error class ValueError.
  There was no completed progress stage or scored transition. The saved
  partial result is not a successful reset+update compatibility result.
- Neither the extra consistency prose nor the update fields were trimmed,
  supplemented or silently reinterpreted. No second episode, replacement
  role reply, changed temperature, profile/endpoint change or batch retry
  was attempted. Parser/schema compatibility did not pass, so the serial
  runner was not invoked and the four formal runs remain unstarted.

Evidence and safety
- raw_compat_v2/ contains exact copies of the 4 probe files and 17
  preparation files, plus PUBLIC_FILE_SHA256.txt listing each copy's byte
  length/SHA-256. All 21 copies were hash-compared to their original local
  capture before commit. The public payloads omit transport auth/endpoint
  fields; private profile bytes, credential values and hidden thinking are
  absent. Error diagnostics in the probe contain safe error classes, not
  private transport exception text. The old Task raw SSE gap remains in
  raw_compat_v1; it was not regenerated.
- The probe result is bound to this scope SHA-256, source/vendor/config,
  profile SHA-256, probe/serial code hashes, certification hash and observed
  role/HTTP evidence. serial_live.run checks those bindings and original
  evidence files, not merely status='passed'. Its old-status-bit negative
  test passed. Here status='failed', so no authorization may be consumed.
- The four-task draft remains historical. No active authorization remains,
  no formal Task container/volume was created, and the successful synthetic
  fixture records its own cleanup. Unrelated monitor __pycache__ was not
  modified. Future live work requires new explicit authorization and a new
  compatibility protocol decision; this failed probe must not be relabeled.

# Synthetic PoS lifecycle fixture

Create `/app/pos-fixture-marker.txt` with exactly `POS_FILE_OK` followed by a newline. Start an HTTP service on `127.0.0.1:19173` that returns status 200. This is a synthetic wiring check, not a benchmark Task.

===== BEGIN raw_v10/normal/synthetic_test.sh bytes=384 sha256=7b4739e7e43e284e923cde3e98e960d15611f2e2bcbdaa844461654537b395be =====
#!/bin/sh
set -eu
test "$(cat /app/pos-fixture-marker.txt)" = POS_FILE_OK
/opt/m4-runtime/python/cpython-3.12.12-linux-x86_64-gnu/bin/python3.12 -c 'import urllib.request; response = urllib.request.build_opener(urllib.request.ProxyHandler({})).open("http://127.0.0.1:19173/", timeout=5); assert response.status == 200'
mkdir -p /logs/verifier
printf '1\n' > /logs/verifier/reward.txt

+class RoleContractTests(unittest.TestCase):
+    def test_historical_two_public_answers_replayed_without_rewriting(self):
+        rows = [json.loads(line) for line in
+            (HERE / 'raw_compat_v1/role_events.jsonl').read_text(
+                encoding='utf-8').splitlines()]
+        original = [row['response'] for row in rows
+                    if row['event'] == 'pos_role_response']
+        self.assertEqual(len(original), 2)
+        for raw in original:
+            with self.subTest(length=len(raw)):
+                with self.assertRaises(json.JSONDecodeError): json.loads(raw)
+                body, status, removed = _adapt_role_json(raw)
+                self.assertEqual(status, 'outer_fence_removed')
+                self.assertEqual(raw, removed['leading'] + removed['opening'] +
+                                 body + removed['closing'] + removed['trailing'])
+                parsed = json.loads(body)
+                BeliefManager._validate_model_payload(parsed, 'initialize')
+
+    def test_only_one_complete_strict_object_fence_is_accepted(self):
+        valid = ['```json\n{"x": 1}\n```',
+                 ' \t\r\n```\r\n{"x": "line\\nvalue"}\r\n```\t\n']
+        for raw in valid:
+            with self.subTest(raw=raw):
+                body, status, boundary = _adapt_role_json(raw)
+                self.assertEqual(status, 'outer_fence_removed')
+                self.assertEqual(raw, boundary['leading'] + boundary['opening'] +
+                                 body + boundary['closing'] + boundary['trailing'])
+        plain = '{"x":1,"x":2}'
+        self.assertEqual(_adapt_role_json(plain), (plain, 'unchanged', None))
+        rejected = [
+            'before\n' + fenced('{"x":1}'), fenced('{"x":1}') + '\nafter',
+            fenced('{"x":1}') + '\n' + fenced('{"y":2}'),
+            fenced('{"x":1} {"y":2}'), fenced('{"x":'),
+            fenced('{"x":1,"x":2}'), fenced('{"x":NaN}'),
+            fenced('{"x":Infinity}'), fenced('{"x":1e999}'),
+            fenced('[1,2]'), fenced('3'), fenced('null'),
+            fenced('{"x":1}') + '\n[!!! Response truncated: max_tokens !!!]',
+            '````json\n{"x":1}\n````',
+        ]
+        for raw in rejected:
+            with self.subTest(raw=raw):
+                self.assertIs(_adapt_role_json(raw)[0], raw)

+    def test_actual_raw_ask_payload_changes_only_pos_role_copy(self):
+        payloads = []
+        class HTTP:
+            status_code = 200
+            headers = {}
+            def __enter__(self): return self
+            def __exit__(self, *args): return False
+            def close(self): pass
+            def json(self): return {'content': [{'type':'text', 'text':'{"issues":[]}'}],
+                                    'usage': {'input_tokens':1, 'output_tokens':1}}
+        def post(_url, **kwargs):
+            payloads.append(kwargs['json'])
+            return HTTP()

+                   'model':'claude-opus-4-8', 'max_tokens':1000,
+                   'temperature':1, 'thinking_type':'adaptive',

+        logger = Recorder()

+        with patch('llmcore.requests.post', side_effect=post):
+            for role in ('belief', 'consistency', 'progress'):
+                self.assertEqual(transport.caller(role)([{'role':'user',
+                    'content':'Synthetic role request.'}]), '{"issues":[]}')
+            task = llmcore.NativeClaudeSession(profile)
+            generator = task.raw_ask([{'role':'user','content':[{'type':'text',
+                'text':'Synthetic Task request.'}]}])
+            with self.assertRaises(StopIteration):
+                while True: next(generator)
+        self.assertEqual(len(payloads), 4)
+        for role, payload in zip(('belief','consistency','progress'), payloads[:3]):
+            self.assertEqual(payload['thinking'], {'type':'disabled'})
+            self.assertEqual(payload['temperature'], 0)
+            self.assertEqual(payload['max_tokens'], 4096 if role == 'belief' else 512)
+        self.assertEqual(payloads[-1]['thinking'], {'type':'adaptive'})
+        self.assertNotIn('temperature', payloads[-1])
+        self.assertEqual(payloads[-1]['max_tokens'], 1000)
+        self.assertEqual(profile['temperature'], 1)
+        self.assertEqual(profile['thinking_type'], 'adaptive')
+        capture = os.environ.get('POS_ROLE_HTTP_CAPTURE')
+        if capture:
+            with Path(capture).open('x', encoding='utf-8', newline='\n') as stream:
+                json.dump({'schema': 'pos-role-http-boundary-offline-v1',
+                           'transport': 'patched_requests_post_no_provider',
+                           'payloads': payloads}, stream, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)
+                stream.write('\n')

 import json
 import os
 from pathlib import Path
+import re
 import sys
 import time
 
 
+ROLE_JSON_CONTRACT_VERSION = 'pos-role-json-object-fence-v1'
+ROLE_THINKING_TYPE = 'disabled'
+ROLE_TEMPERATURE = 0
+ROLE_MAX_TOKENS = {'belief': 4096, 'consistency': 512, 'progress': 512}
+_JSON_FENCE = re.compile(
+    r'(?P<leading>[ \t\r\n]*)(?P<opening>```(?:json)?\r?\n)'
+    r'(?P<body>.*?)(?P<closing>\r?\n```)(?P<trailing>[ \t\r\n]*)',
+    re.DOTALL,
+)
+
+
+def _strict_object(raw):
+    """Check only the newly accepted fenced path; upstream owns all schemas."""
+    def unique(pairs):
+        result = {}
+        for key, value in pairs:
+            if key in result:
+                raise ValueError('duplicate JSON key')
+            result[key] = value
+        return result
+    def reject_constant(_value):
+        raise ValueError('nonstandard JSON constant')
+    def finite_float(value):
+        number = float(value)
+        if not (float('-inf') < number < float('inf')):
+            raise ValueError('nonfinite JSON number')
+        return number
+    value = json.loads(raw, object_pairs_hook=unique,
+                       parse_constant=reject_constant, parse_float=finite_float)
+    if not isinstance(value, dict):
+        raise ValueError('JSON value is not an object')
+
+
+def _adapt_role_json(raw):
+    """Remove one enumerated outer fence only around one strict JSON object."""
+    match = _JSON_FENCE.fullmatch(raw)
+    if match is None:
+        return raw, 'unchanged', None
+    body = match.group('body')
+    try:
+        _strict_object(body)
+    except (ValueError, TypeError, RecursionError):
+        return raw, 'fence_rejected', None
+    boundary = {name: match.group(name) for name in
+                ('leading', 'opening', 'closing', 'trailing')}
+    return body, 'outer_fence_removed', boundary
+
+
 def _json(value):
     # GA's shipped tool-schema asset contains an escaped lone surrogate.
     # JSON ASCII escaping preserves its exact code point across the pipe.

                 self.archive.log('pos_role_response', **record)
-                return response
+                parser_input, transform, boundary = _adapt_role_json(response)
+                self.archive.log('pos_role_json_adapter',
+                                 contract_version=ROLE_JSON_CONTRACT_VERSION,
+                                 role=role, attempt=record['attempt'],
+                                 call_identity=f"{role}:{record['attempt']}",
+                                 transform=transform, raw_response=response,
+                                 raw_sha256=hashlib.sha256(response.encode('utf-8')).hexdigest(),
+                                 parser_input=parser_input,
+                                 parser_input_sha256=hashlib.sha256(
+                                     parser_input.encode('utf-8')).hexdigest(),
+                                 removed_boundary=boundary)
+                return parser_input
             except BaseException as error:
                 record.update(status='failed', error_type=type(error).__name__,
                               error=str(error), elapsed_seconds=time.monotonic() - started)
